[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[default]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1384560721}[命令并不能保证接口下的所有命令都能恢复到缺省情况，某些命令可能会由于不满足必备条件而恢复失败。因此，执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[default]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令后建议通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[display this]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令确认执行效果。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_622229197}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1477801289}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[恢复为缺省配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x68026238}

[\[Sysname\] interface vlan-interface 1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Vlan-interface1\] default]{lang="EN-US"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- description**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1175753196}[命令用来配置当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的描述信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2143123706}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x487871064}

[**[description]{lang="EN-US"}***[ text]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1747106311}

[**[undo description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2075097069}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1605277649}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1634053960}[的描述信息为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN *vlan-id*]{lang="EN-US"}["，其中]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的四位数编号，如果该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号不足四位，则会在编号前增加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，补齐四位。例如，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[的描述信息为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 0100]{lang="EN-US"}["；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的描述信息为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的接口名，如"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Vlan-interface1 Interface]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x166622789}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x441513276}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1335205015}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2142927098}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_478886766}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1205780181}

[*[text]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1724636287}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的描述信息，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，可以包含字母（区分大小写）、数字、特殊字符（包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}[\~ ! @ \# \$ % \^ & \* ( ) - \_ + = { } \[ \] \| \\ : ; \" \' \< \> , . /]{lang="EN-US"}[）、空格以及符合]{style="font-family:宋体"}[unicode]{lang="EN-US"}[编码规范的其他文字和符号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_198260641}

[[当设备上配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1331470235}[较多时，用户可以根据功能或者连接情况为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口配置特定的描述信息，以便记忆和管理]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1827634471}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1833148117}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[的描述信息配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[sales-private]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2142992634}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan2\] description sales-private]{lang="EN-US"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- display interface vlan-interface**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **interface vlan-interface**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1091807399}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的相关信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2142796026}

[**[display interface]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan-interface** \[ *interface-number* \] \[ **brief** \[ **description** \| **down** \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2032530385}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1964517678}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1816774372}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1490692297}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1043723297}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1121439443}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2064665803}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1639608684}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2142861562}

[**[vlan-interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2032595921}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的相关信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_990420915}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的编号，显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的信息。不指定该参数时，将显示已创建的所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[brief]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1407736618}[：显示接口的概要信息。不指定该参数时，将显示接口的详细信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_766381248}[：用来显示用户配置的接口的全部描述信息。如果某接口的描述信息超过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[27]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符，指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[brief]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数而不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时，只显示描述信息中的前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[27]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符，超出部分不显示；指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时，可以显示全部描述信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[down]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x596103737}[：显示当前物理状态为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的接口的信息以及]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的原因。不指定该参数时，将不会根据接口物理状态来过滤显示信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1157637243}

[[Link: ADM - administratively down; Stby - standby]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_285009122}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果某接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220547258}[Link]{lang="EN-US"}[属性值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ADM]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示该接口被管理员手工关闭了，需要在该接口下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令才能恢复端口本身的物理状态]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果某接口的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Link]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_205098147}[属性值为"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Stby]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示该接口是一个备份接口，使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[display ]{lang="EN-US"}[interface-backup]{lang="EN-US"}[ state]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令可以查看该备份接口对应的主接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果某接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Protocol]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1924540869}[属性值中带有"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[(s)]{lang="EN-US"}["字符串，则表示该接口的数据链路层协议状态显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[UP]{lang="EN-US"}[，但实际可能没有对应的链路，或者对应的链路不是永久存在而是按需建立的]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- display vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x694865387}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的相关信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_691176577}

[**[display]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan** \[ *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] \| **all** \| **dynamic** \| **reserved** \| **static** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1297267609}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_607571377}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1527608557}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x842849653}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1945818304}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220743865}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1382833994}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x6487594}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2098312185}

[*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1636724736}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[ **to** *vlan-id2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2015314506}[：显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ID]{lang="EN-US"}[在指定范围内的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*[为指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1467430291}[：显示除保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[外的其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dynamic]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1888803561}[：显示系统动态创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的数量和编号。动态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是指通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MVRP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议生成或通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RADIUS]{lang="EN-US"}[服务器下发的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[reserved]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1544289835}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[显示系统保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是设备根据功能实现的需要预留的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[由协议模块来指定，为协议模块服务，用户不能对保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[进行任何操作。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220809401}[：显示系统静态创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的数量和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[编号。静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是指通过命令行手工创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_802941653}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1385522126}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x530346601}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口之前，对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须已经存在，否则将不能创建指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2032137168}[及在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN interface]{lang="EN-US"}[下配置了三层互通的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不能创建对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。有关]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的详细介绍，请参见"二层技术]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[-]{lang="EN-US"}[以太网交换配置指导"中的"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}["；有关]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的详细介绍，请参见"二层技术]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[-]{lang="EN-US"}[以太网交换配置指导"中的"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Private VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1974794523}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1886127892}[创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[并进入视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- mtu**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1947518123}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x360092710}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220809404}

[**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *size*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1350179663}

[**[undo mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_614683953}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1375944911}

[[本命令的缺省情况与设备型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x655096243}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2038155778}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1419593955}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2061523282}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x106304535}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1182385008}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220612796}

[*[size]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1382017751}[：表示接口允许通过的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Maximum Transmission Unit]{lang="EN-US"}[，最大传输单元）值的大小，单位为字节。不同型号的设备支持的取值范围不同，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x824322746}

[[如果当前接口同时配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x211883471}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ip mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，则设备会以]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ip mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令配置的接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值对报文进行分片，不会再按照]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值对报文进行分片。有关]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ip mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的详细介绍，请参见"三层技术]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}[业务命令参考"中的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[性能优化"。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1021015373}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1598630380}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1492]{lang="EN-US"}[字节。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- name**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[name]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1335797359}[命令用来指定当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的名称。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo name]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x131942674}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1821953065}

[**[name ]{lang="EN-US"}***[text]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_46436492}

[**[undo name]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2065675025}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_209563537}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2027574718}[的名称为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN *vlan-id*]{lang="EN-US"}["，其中]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[为该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的四位数编号，如果该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号不足四位，则会在编号前增加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[，补齐四位。例如，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[的名称为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 0100]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1767032690}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220481724}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1362004491}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x822299330}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1470241318}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1138376531}

[*[text]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x240885299}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的描述信息，可以包含字母（区分大小写）、数字、特殊字符（包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}[\~ ! @ \# \$ % \^ & \* ( ) - \_ + = { } \[ \] \| \\ : ; \" \' \< \> , . /]{lang="EN-US"}[）、空格以及符合]{style="font-family:宋体"}[unicode]{lang="EN-US"}[编码规范的其他文字和符号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1248742392}

[[当设备上配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1X]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x954751828}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址认证功能后，可以通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RADIUS]{lang="EN-US"}[服务器来对认证通过的端口下发]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。某些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RADIUS]{lang="EN-US"}[服务器可以向设备发送需要下发的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[编号或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[名称，当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[数量很多的时候，使用名称可以更明确的定位]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1046773267}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220547260}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[的名称为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[test vlan]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_204573856}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan2\] name test vlan]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1013588972}

:::

[[在某些情况下，需要统计一定时间内某接口的流量，这就需要在统计开始前清除该接口原有的统计信息，重新进行统计。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x730231500}

宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1539254944}[，则清除指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:

宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220416188}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220285116}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果未手工关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1754505956}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口，此时]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口状态受]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中端口状态的影响，即：当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中所有以太网端口状态均为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态，即关闭状态；当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中有一个或一个以上的以太网端口处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态时，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果手工关闭]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_141090145}[接口，则]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的状态始终为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Administratively]{lang="EN-US"}[），不受]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中端口状态的影响。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1271304040}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口参数前，为了避免配置过程中对网络造成影响，建议先使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令手工关闭接口，之后再配置参数。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[配置完成后，使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令取消手工关闭接口，使配置的参数生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1416558702}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口出现故障时，可以使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令手工关闭接口，然后再使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令取消手工关闭接口，这样有可能使接口恢复正常。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[关闭和打开]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1434509787}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口对于属于这个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的任何一个以太网端口本身都不起作用，以太网端口的状态不随]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口状态的改变而改变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1074152637}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1982012957}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[关闭后再重新打开。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] interface vlan-interface 2]{lang="EN-US"}

**VLAN \-- VLAN配置命令 \-- vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1606959788}[命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[用来创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图。如果指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[已创建，则直接进入该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id1*]{lang="EN-US"}**[ to]{lang="EN-US"}***[ vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x571921641}[命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[用来批量创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*[之间的所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[除外。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vlan all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1388416580}[命令用来批量创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ]{lang="EN-US"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1493131308}[命令用来删除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1376656397}

[**[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] *\|* **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x124603140}

[**[undo vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}*[{ *vlan-id1* \[]{lang="EN-US"}[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] *\|* **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1433362287}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2027617900}

[[系统只有一个缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1514320646}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220809403}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1349983055}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_176596144}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1528379581}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_537083745}

[*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x455775843}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[ **to** *vlan-id2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1058690143}[：指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号范围。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_627582067}[：除保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[外的其他]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[当设备允许创建的最大]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[数小于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[时，不支持该参数。本参数的支持情况与设备型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220612795}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1382214359}[为系统缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，用户不能创建和删除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[保留]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1625208285}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是系统为实现特定功能预留的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，用户也不能手工创建和删除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[动态学习到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1642911643}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，以及被其他应用锁定不让删除的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，都不能使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令直接删除。只有将相关配置删除之后，才能删除相应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_80369305}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x314636754}[创建]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[，并进入该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[*[interface-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1823329523}[：以太网接口列表。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-list ]{lang="EN-US"}*[= { *interface-type interface-number1* \[ **to** *interface-type interface-number2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，其中]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为端口类型和端口编号，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-number2]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-number1]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2068624008}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过本命令只能将]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1040930668}[端口加入到]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中，不能将]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口加入到]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[设备上的所有端口的缺省链路类型都是]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x858365622}[类型，但用户可以自行切换端口类型，具体配置可参考命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220285115}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1754309348}[向]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN2]{lang="EN-US"}[中添加端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x324381324}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan2\] port gigabitethernet 1/0/1 to gigabitethernet 1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x271551084}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2107936205}
:::

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port access vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port access vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x405297425}[命令用来将当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[端口加入到指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port access vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_831016591}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_813202589}

[**[port access vlan ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220743870}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **port** **access vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1383161675}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1528949745}

[[所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_69159983}[端口都属于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1402753145}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1964993626}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x572411788}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1094349333}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x51498213}

[*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220809406}[：指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须是设备上已创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，否则，该命令执行失败。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1350310735}

[[在将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1026303287}[端口加入到指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[之前，该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须已经存在。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1855433936}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1594587599}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[端口加入到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[中。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 3]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan3\] quit]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port access vlan 3]{lang="EN-US"}

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port hybrid pvid**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port hybrid pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1381362391}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port hybrid pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x674460072}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_31720069}

[**[port hybrid pvid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x226762582}

[**[undo port hybrid pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x200480071}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x705564313}

[[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_486065069}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为该端口在链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[时的所属]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2047328376}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220678334}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1335928431}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1735083829}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1810060076}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1384353621}

[*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1065926616}[：指定接口的缺省的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1380784613}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1477228162}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令删除端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[后，端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[配置不会改变，即可以使用已经不存在的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[作为缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[建议本机]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2115373566}[端口的缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和相连的对端交换机的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[保持一致。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_686529070}[后，必须使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}[命令配置允许缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文通过，出接口才能转发缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1299021034}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1663835117}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型）的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[，并允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan100\] quit]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port link-type hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port hybrid pvid vlan 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port hybrid vlan 100 untagged]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1209214126}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port hybrid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1334351884}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220547262}

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port hybrid vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port hybrid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1126739776}[命令用来允许指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port hybrid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1014144886}[命令用来禁止指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2081281898}

[**[port hybrid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list* { **tagged** \| **untagged** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x319430829}

[**[undo port hybrid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x184429921}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1225921711}

[[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1890559829}[端口只允许该端口在链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[时的所属]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文以]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x807977323}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220350654}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2128477956}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x640869678}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_412232515}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1973338344}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1821601714}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口允许通过的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[范围。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须是设备上已创建的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，否则，该命令执行失败。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[tagged]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_66800199}[：该端口在转发指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[报文时将携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[untagged]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_480392851}[：该端口在转发指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[报文时将去掉]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x217140619}

[[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1047908891}[端口允许多个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过。如果多次使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}[命令，那么]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口上允许通过的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是这些]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[的合集。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_944006152}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1386490373}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[50]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[通过，并且发送这些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文时携带]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_231585519}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port link-type hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port hybrid vlan 2 4 50 to 100 tagged]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1013033610}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1716339459}

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port link-type**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220219582}[命令用来配置当前端口的链路类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x608560607}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x706219710}

[**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **access** \| **hybrid** \| **trunk** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1889339955}

[**[undo port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1960396629}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1253379987}

[[所有端口的链路类型均为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x848669335}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1522019072}

[**[access]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1940835161}[：配置端口的链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1311535525}[：配置端口的链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[trunk]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1967350353}[：配置端口的链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x599081724}[端口和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[端口之间不能直接切换，只能先设为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，再配置为其他类型端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1383620426}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x826968274}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port link-type trunk]{lang="EN-US"}

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port trunk permit vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_26744154}[命令用来允许指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_393118398}[命令用来禁止指定的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_427196685}

[**[port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *vlan-id-list* \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1790584232}

[**[undo port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *vlan-id-list* \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220809405}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1350114127}

[[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x152415612}[端口只允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x896226431}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2055336266}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x256948068}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1202558912}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_734316576}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1962685346}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1493459374}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口允许通过的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[范围。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220612797}[：表示允许所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口。建议用户谨慎使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port trunk permit vlan all]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，以防止未授权]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的用户通过该端口访问受限资源。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1382083287}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x439352625}[端口可以允许多个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[如果多次执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，那么]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口上允许通过的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是这些]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-]{lang="EN-US"}[id-]{lang="EN-US"}[list]{lang="EN-US"}*[的集合。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1131951254}[端口发送出去的报文，只有缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文不带]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[，其他]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文均会保留]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x372018696}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x918528838}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[50]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220678333}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port link-type trunk]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port trunk permit vlan 2 4 50 to 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1335862895}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port link-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1081343312}
:::

**VLAN \-- 基于端口的VLAN配置命令 \-- port trunk pvid**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port trunk pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2042078996}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1424975813}**[port trunk pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1942558897}

[**[port trunk pvid vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1734481900}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x876506241}**[port trunk pvid]{lang="EN-US"}**

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_786021894}

[[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220481725}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1362070027}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_661320101}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1306730940}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_703996813}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_663069017}

[*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1926629780}[：指定接口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x220547261}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_204508320}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令删除端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[后，端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[配置不会改变，即使用已经不存在的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[作为缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本端设备]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2137440954}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ID]{lang="EN-US"}[和相连的对端设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ID]{lang="EN-US"}[必须一致，否则报文将不能正确传输。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1035536667}[后，必须使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port trunk permit vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令配置允许缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文通过，出接口才能转发缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的报文。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1112383619}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_820750200}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk]{lang="EN-US"}[类型）的缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[，并允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 100]{lang="EN-US"}[通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mac-address ]{lang="EN-US"}***[mac-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2125410820}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表项，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mac-address]{lang="PT-BR"}*[格式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mask]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *mac-mask*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1253693467}[：显示指定范围的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mac-mask]{lang="PT-BR"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[二进制时高位必须为连续]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="PT-BR"}[，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[十六进制时高位必须为连续]{style="font-family:宋体"}[F]{lang="PT-BR"}[。缺省十六进制值为全]{style="font-family:宋体"}[F]{lang="EN-US"}[。该参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1301658687}[：显示静态配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1126142989}

[**[mac-address ]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1480234790}*[mac-address]{lang="PT-BR"}*[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mac-address]{lang="PT-BR"}*[格式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="PT-BR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[不支持组播]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址和全]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="PT-BR"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址。在配置时，用户可以省去]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址中每段开头的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}["，例如输入"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[f-e2-1]{lang="EN-US"}["即表示输入的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[000f-00e2-0001]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mask]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_939215383}*[ mac-mask]{lang="PT-BR"}*[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址的掩码]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mac-mask]{lang="PT-BR"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[二进制时高位必须为连续]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="PT-BR"}[，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[十六进制时高位必须为连续]{style="font-family:宋体"}[F]{lang="PT-BR"}[。缺省十六进制值为全]{style="font-family:宋体"}[F]{lang="EN-US"}[。该参数的支持情况与设备型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vlan ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2105284804}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dot1q]{lang="EN-US"}[ ]{lang="EN-US"}***[priority]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1343547795}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级，取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。取值越大，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级越高。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1176763445}[：表示删除所有的静态]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x300308155}

[[设备维护两张]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x596365884}[表，一张是通过指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mask]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表，该表里的表项描述的是一类]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级之间的关系；一张是不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mask]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数配置的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表，该表里的表项描述的是单个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[、优先级之间的关系。根据用户的配置，系统将自动在这两张]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表里添加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[删除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- 基于MAC的VLAN配置命令 \-- vlan precedence**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[vlan precedence]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_109253638}[命令用来配置当前接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[优先匹配方式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo vlan precedence]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1130938040}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_75412761}

[**[vlan precedence]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **mac-vlan** \| **ip-subnet-vlan** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2019653551}

[**[undo vlan precedence]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_233282330}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1538882605}

[[对于基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1374354760}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，优先根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址来匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_848496532}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x595972668}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x167625391}

[[network-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1971951468}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1705155731}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x308974894}

[**[mac-vlan]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_61654076}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[表示优先根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址来匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ip-subnet-vlan]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1707314333}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[表示优先根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="PT-BR"}[子网来匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1447335018}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本命令只对基于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_589227561}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和基于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[有效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mac-vlan trigger enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1012292165}[命令使能]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的动态触发功能后，建议用户在配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[匹配优先级时，使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[vlan precedence mac-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，使报文优先]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[根据]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址来匹配]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[vlan precedence ip-subnet-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令不能生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x596038204}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x650346392}[配置接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernt1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[优先根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址来匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] vlan precedence mac-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}

[*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873739752}[：显示指定的子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1* **to** *vlan*-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_714218296}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[范围内的子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*[为指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- 基于IP子网的VLAN配置命令 \-- ip-subnet-vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[ip-subnet-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x176002093}[命令用来配置当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ip-subnet]{lang="EN-US"}[-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1667820232}[命令用于取消当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873739753}

[**[ip-subnet-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *ip-subnet-index* \] **ip** *ip-address* \[ *mask* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_714283832}

[**[undo ip-subnet-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *ip-subnet-index* \[ **to** *ip-subnet-end* \] \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2024132654}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873674217}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x897775824}[没有与任何]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2073132005}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1874132969}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1377690080}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1317997089}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1769145551}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1874067433}

[*[ip-subnet-index]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1839039473}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网索引值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。子网索引可以由用户指定，也可由系统根据]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[关联的先后顺序自动编号产生。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ip ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ip-address ]{lang="EN-US"}*[\[ *mask* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_726224050}[：作为子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[划分依据的源]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址或网络地址，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示源]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址或网络地址，采用点分十进制格式；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mask]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示子网掩码，采用点分十进制格式，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255.255.255.0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[to]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ip-subnet-end]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873608678}[：用来确定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网索引的范围。其中]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-subnet-end]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网索引的终止值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，其值必须大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-subnet-index]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x872319253}[：取消]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873543142}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1842325755}[关联的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[网段或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址不允许是组播网段或组播地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2008606245}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1873477606}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[配置为基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[192.168.1.0/24]{lang="EN-US"}[网段进行关联，使得源地址为该网段的报文可以在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[中传送。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1699251318}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 3]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan3\] ip-subnet-vlan ip 192.168.1.0 255.255.255.0]{lang="EN-US"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **port hybrid ip-subnet-vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x75222015}[命令用于取消当前端口与子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_714349368}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x897710288}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_725100112}

[**[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_59414254}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[执行本命令时，需保证当前端口的链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1099893675}[，并允许子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过，且子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下存在与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[子网或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址关联的配置，该命令才实际生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1839105009}[将端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[与子网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- 基于协议的VLAN配置命令 \-- display protocol-vlan vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display protocol-vlan vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x742419403}[命令用于显示指定的或所有协议]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_833563328}

[**[display protocol-vlan vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}*[{ *vlan*]{lang="EN-US"}[-*id1* \[ **to** *vlan*-*id2* \] *\|* **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855209137}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1281076232}

[*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x106012503}[：显示指定的协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1* **to** *vlan*-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855274672}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[范围内的协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id2]{lang="EN-US"}*[为指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_825630580}[：显示所有协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**VLAN \-- 基于协议的VLAN配置命令 \-- port hybrid protocol-vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port hybrid protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x854750384}[命令用来配置当前端口与协议]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **port hybrid protocol-vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1115819340}[命令用于取消当前端口与协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x313473581}

[**[port hybrid protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **vlan** *vlan-id* { *protocol-index* \[ **to** *protocol-end* \] \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_284980436}

[**[undo hybrid protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **vlan** *vlan-id* { *protocol-index* \[ **to** *protocol-end* \] \| **all** } \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x854815920}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1281010696}

[[端口没有与任何协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2029160599}[关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855274675}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_825565044}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2125741660}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855340211}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1910377943}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_279423059}

[**[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan*-*id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2009025520}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol-index]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855405747}[：协议模板索引，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[to]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_57781126}[：协议模板索引的范围。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol-end]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_906890749}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[协议模板索引终止值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，其值必须大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[protocol-index]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855471283}[：所有协议模板。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x342262282}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_792580203}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行本命令时，需确保当前端口的链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1971116178}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[，并允许协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过，且协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下存在与协议模板关联的配置，本命令才实际生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **port hybrid protocol-vlan**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_792645739}[命令时，如果同时指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则表示取消当前端口与指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[绑定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[所有协议模板的关联；如果仅指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则表示取消当前端口与所有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[绑定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[所有协议模板的关联。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x626014628}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855012531}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[与协议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[中的协议模板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}
