**VLAN \-- 基于协议的VLAN配置命令 \-- protocol-vlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855143603}[命令用来配置当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与指定的协议模板关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ]{lang="EN-US"}[protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x742550475}[命令用于取消当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与协议模板的关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2036732211}

[**[protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *protocol-index* \] { **at** \| **ipv4** \| **ipv6** \| **ipx** { **ethernetii** *\|* **llc** \| **raw** *\|* **snap** } \| **mode** { **ethernetii** **etype** *etype-id* \| **llc** { **dsap** *dsap-id* \[ **ssap** *ssap-id* \] \| **ssap** *ssap-id* } \| **snap** **etype** *etype-id* } }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855209139}

[**[undo protocol-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *protocol-index* \[ **to** *protocol-end* \] \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1592992289}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2012320169}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1995292215}[没有与任何协议模板关联。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x854750387}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1115622732}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_975732818}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x854815923}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1281207304}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_290706821}

[**[at]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1300282434}[：基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AT]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[AppleTalk]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Apple]{lang="EN-US"}[计算机网络协议）协议的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ipv4]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855274674}[：基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ipv6]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_825499508}[：基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ipx]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x191104846}[：基于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPX]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，可包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ethernetii]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[llc]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[raw]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[snap]{lang="EN-US"}**[四种封装类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855340210}[：配置自定义协议模板，可包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ethernetii]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[llc]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[snap]{lang="EN-US"}**[三种封装类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ethernetii]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **etype** *etype-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1910312407}[：匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ethernet II]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式及相应的协议类型值。其中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[etype-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示入报文的协议类型值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0600]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[（不包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0800]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x809B]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x8137]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x86DD]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[llc]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x107964318}[：匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LLC]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[dsap ]{lang="EN-US"}***[dsap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_431866932}[：目的服务接入点，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFF]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ssap ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ssap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855405746}[：源服务接入点，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x00]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFF]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[snap]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **etype** *etype-id*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_57846662}[：匹配]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[SNAP]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式及相应的协议类型值。其中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[etype-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示入报文的以太网类型，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0600]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[（不包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x8137]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol-index]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1392357267}[：协议索引，用来标识与当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[绑定的协议模版，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果不指定该参数，则系统会自动分配一个索引值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[to]{lang="EN-US"}***[ protocol-end]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855471282}[：用来确定协议索引的范围。其中]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[protocol-end]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[协议索引终止值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，其值必须大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[protocol-index]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol-end]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x342327818}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[协议索引终止值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[65535]{lang="EN-US"}[，必须大于等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[protocol-index]{lang="EN-US"}*[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_807455207}[：与当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[绑定的所有协议。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1600272254}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855012530}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[由于]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x721549200}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[协议与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议关系密切，建议用户将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[协议和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议绑定到同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，并且关联到相同的端口，避免因]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP]{lang="EN-US"}[报文与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[报文未划分到同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，而造成无法正常通信的情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mode ethernetii etype]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1517938005}[关键字进行配置时，不允许将]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[etype-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值配置为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0800]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x809B]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x8137]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x86DD]{lang="EN-US"}[，因为上述取值分别与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[AppleTalk]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPX]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[协议模板相同。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x855078066}**[mode llc]{lang="EN-US"}**[关键字进行配置时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[dsap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ssap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值不允许同时为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xE0]{lang="EN-US"}[（代表]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPX]{lang="EN-US"}[报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LLC]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式）、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xFF]{lang="EN-US"}[（代表]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPX]{lang="EN-US"}[报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[raw]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式）、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xAA]{lang="EN-US"}[（代表]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SNAP]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式）；如果只配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[dsap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ssap-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[中的一个，系统会将另一参数的值自动配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0xAA]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mode snap etype]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1934780805}[关键字进行配置时，不允许将]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[etype-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值配置为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x8137]{lang="EN-US"}[，因为该值与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPX]{lang="EN-US"}[协议协议模板相同。当]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[etype-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值取]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0800]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x809B]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[0x86DD]{lang="EN-US"}[时，其对应的协议]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[模板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[分别为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[AppleTalk]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x59013067}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1669473694}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[报文和采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ethernet II]{lang="EN-US"}[封装格式的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP]{lang="EN-US"}[报文（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议的协议代码为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0x0806]{lang="EN-US"}[）划分到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[中传输。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x854750389}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[（取值范围与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准），]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[TENTATIVE\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x923968931}[：该状态为地址初始化状态，此时该地址可能正在进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测或准备进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测，处于该状态的地址不能作为报文的源地址或者目的地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[DUPLICATE\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1288611476}[：该状态表明地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测已经结束，由于该地址在链路上不唯一，因此不能使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[PREFERRED\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_388902461}[：该状态表明地址处于首选生命期以内。该状态的地址可以作为报文的源地址或者目的地址。为该状态时，不显示地址的状态标识]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[DEPRECATED\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_388967997}[：该状态表明地址超过首选生命期，但是在有效生命期以内。该状态地址有效，但不应作为新建连接报文的源地址，目的地址是该地址的报文还可以被正常处理]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**Super VLAN \-- Super VLAN配置命令 \-- subvlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[subvlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1318921399}[命令用来建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo subvlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1031114604}[命令用来解除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1450032496}

[**[subvlan ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x888403431}

[**[undo subvlan ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ *vlan-id-list* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_412012624}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345667755}

[[未建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2089550712}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x341048220}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_68597060}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1496360762}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1247388458}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x90707762}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x226079151}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_955324898}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ *=* { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[。其中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN ID]{lang="EN-US"}[，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1539106746}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345864363}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系]{style="font-family:宋体"}[前，指定的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须已经创建。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在建立了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x125734808}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系后，仍可以向]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中添加和删除接口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo subvlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869599895}[命令时，如果不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则解除当前]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与所有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系；如果指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则解除当前]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1276597446}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2077129575}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN 10]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**Super VLAN \-- Super VLAN配置命令 \-- supervlan**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[supervlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1338185649}[命令用来配置当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo supervlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_850381265}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x774679225}

[**[supervlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_619047243}

[**[undo supervlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1165840753}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1482992953}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x867425812}[类型不为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1319933878}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1270956084}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345340076}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x134528008}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1161987916}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1513915107}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果某个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1433443890}[被指定为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则该]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[建议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[被指定为某个端口的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Guest VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[/Auth-Fail VLAN/Critical VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；同样，如果某个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[被指定为某个端口的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Guest VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[/Auth-Fail VLAN/Critical VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则该]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[建议]{style="font-family:宋体"}[被指定为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Guest VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[/Auth-Fail VLAN/Critical VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的相关内容请参见"安全配置指导"中的"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1X]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345274540}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口下配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VRRP]{lang="EN-US"}[功能后，会对网络性能造成影响，建议不要这样配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x999388262}[下可以配置二层组播功能，但是]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[由于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中没有物理端口，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[该配置将不会生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_250311088}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1523575025}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1980165907}

[\[Sysname\] vlan 2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-vlan2\] supervlan]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[subvlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1383975515}

[*[primary-vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1632815705}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。如果未指定该参数，则显示所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和其对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[可能的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1703949613}[地址状态及含义如下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[TENTATIVE\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1632488024}[：该状态为地址初始化状态，此时该地址可能正在进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测或准备进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测，处于该状态的地址不能作为报文的源地址或者目的地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[DUPLICATE\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1989845849}[：该状态表明地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DAD]{lang="EN-US"}[检测已经结束，由于该地址在链路上不唯一，因此不能使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[PREFERRED\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1045265594}[：该状态表明地址处于首选生命期以内。该状态的地址可以作为报文的源地址或者目的地址。为该状态时，不显示地址的状态标识]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[\[DEPRECATED\]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x974461378}[：该状态表明地址超过首选生命期，但是在有效生命期以内。该状态地址有效，但不应作为新建连接报文的源地址，目的地址是该地址的报文还可以被正常处理]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- port private-vlan host**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_960674620}[命令用来配置端口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1380330214}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1262974870}

[**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x477485804}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1979359152}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x863563918}

[[端口不工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_167083767}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x900826356}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345405609}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1406788993}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1114163290}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1100916526}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1975585783}

[[端口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x60911043}[模式时，若端口已经加入了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则端口也会同步加入和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[有映射关系的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_446099679}[命令恢复缺省情况时，端口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[属性（包括端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[以及端口的链路类型、端口缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[）不做改变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1311114983}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1296271794}[命令时，当端口同步加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则会配置端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为端口加入的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk/Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则端口的链路类型及缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1199900187}[命令时，当端口的链路类型最终为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，端口同步加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[时，若此前端口有以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置，则保持原有配置；否则端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可以在执行完]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345602217}[命令后，再配置端口加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时，该命令的功能依然生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x923051427}[命令与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}[及]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令互斥。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x296454895}

[[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1045265596}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。配置链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，将端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x974330306}[配置链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[\[]{lang="EN-US"}[Sysname]{lang="EN-US"}[-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] ]{lang="EN-US"}[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\[]{lang="EN-US"}[Sysname]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1808337780}[-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] ]{lang="EN-US"}[port access vlan 20]{lang="EN-US"}

[\#]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1174907983}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- port private-vlan promiscuous**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2062553172}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x571049988}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345733289}

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id* **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1795149522}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x673300708}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1420688835}

[[端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2073554212}[中不工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_469006400}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_924980757}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1361582948}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_612056155}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1037137364}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345667753}

[*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2089943928}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。其中，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[虽然在取值范围中，但不能配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2118116843}

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1560492826}[命令同时会将端口加入指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。此时若指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且存在和指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[有映射关系的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则端口也会同步加入这些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2019048575}[命令恢复缺省情况时，端口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[属性（包括端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[以及端口的链路类型、缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置）不做改变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_187866499}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x597752008}[命令时，若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则会配置端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk/Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则端口的链路类型及缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1051181807}[命令时，当端口的链路类型最终为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，端口加入对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（包括指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[及同步加入的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[）时，若端口此前存在以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入某些对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置，则在保持原有配置的基础上，端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入其他对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；否则端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入所有对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。即自动产生的以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置不会覆盖原有以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345864361}[命令时，若端口已配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，则系统会自动先执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，再执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}[命令。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x125603736}[命令时，若端口已配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[在某]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[则取消端口加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可以在执行完]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1312792728}[命令后，再配置指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时，该命令的功能依然生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port private-vlan promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_302608128}[命令与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**[及]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令互斥。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1228627967}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- port private-vlan trunk promiscuous**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1063932494}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x832217302}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[退出]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_75203616}

[**[port private-vlan ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_337025015}

[**[undo port ]{lang="EN-US"}[private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list* **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1901014078}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1442580901}

[[端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x939376463}[中不工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345274538}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_249786793}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2054570046}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_324865409}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2108320033}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_650712775}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_953836505}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的范围。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。其中，系统缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[）虽然在取值范围中，但不能配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_632679489}

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_40452831}[命令同时会将端口加入指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。此时若指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且存在和指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[有映射关系的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则端口也会同步加入这些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x966082851}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中退出]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，但端口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[属性（包括端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[以及端口的链路类型、缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置）不做改变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345471146}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_245544937}[命令时，若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则会配置端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变；若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk/Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则端口的链路类型及缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1199458640}[命令时，当端口的链路类型最终为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，端口加入对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（包括指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[及同步加入的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[）时，若端口此前存在以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入某些对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置，则在保持原有配置的基础上，端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入其他对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；否则端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入所有对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。即自动产生的以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置不会覆盖原有以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345405610}[命令时，若已配置端口在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-]{lang="EN-US"}[id-]{lang="EN-US"}[list]{lang="EN-US"}*[中工作在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，则取消端口加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-]{lang="EN-US"}[id-]{lang="EN-US"}[list]{lang="EN-US"}*[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可以在执行完]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk promiscuous**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1407247744}[命令后，再配置指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时，该命令的功能依然生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1171358209}[命令与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}***[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk ]{lang="EN-US"}[secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[及]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令互斥。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](VLAN命令.files/image002.png){width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_497324114}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuou]{lang="EN-US"}[s]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1954724251}[命令适用于需要多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过上行端口的情况，配置该命令后多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[携带]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[通过上行端口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}***[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**[适用于只有一个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过上行端口的情况，配置该命令后]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不带]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[通过上行端口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_470920111}

[[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1045265592}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[关联，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 30]{lang="EN-US"}[关联。配置链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，然后取消该配置。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x974068162}[配置链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1746855495}[取消]{style="font-family:宋体"}[端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[中的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk ]{lang="EN-US"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- port private-vlan trunk secondary**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_152192122}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869468827}[命令用来配置端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[退出]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869534363}

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list* **trunk secondary**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1464931524}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list* **trunk secondary**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869337755}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_808249142}

[[端口在指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869403291}[中不工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_870255259}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x518433808}[二层聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_870320795}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869730970}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2102044381}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869796506}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869599898}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的范围。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。其中，系统缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[）虽然在取值范围中，但不能配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x38416772}

[**[port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **trunk secondary**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869665434}[命令同时会将端口加入指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。此时若指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且存在和指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[有映射关系的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，则端口也会同步加入这些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869468826}[命令将指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[退出]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，但端口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[关系（包括端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[以及端口的链路类型、缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置）不做改变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1633891019}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[工作在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_869337754}[模式的端口，对于一个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，只能加入其中的一个，但可以分别加入多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_870255258}[命令时，当端口同步加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则会配置端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变；若此前端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk/Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则端口的链路类型及缺省]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不做改变。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_870320794}[命令时，当端口的链路类型最终为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，端口加入对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（包括指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[及同步加入的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[）时，若端口此前存在以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入某些对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置，则在保持原有配置的基础上，端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入其他对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[；否则端口会以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入所有对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。即自动产生的以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged]{lang="EN-US"}[方式加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置不会覆盖原有以]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Tagged/Untagged]{lang="EN-US"}[加入]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行完]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1859086852}[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[后，再]{style="font-family:宋体"}[执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令时，若端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则端口加入指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置不做改变；若端口的链路类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Trunk/Hybrid]{lang="EN-US"}[类型，则取消端口加入指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可以在执行完]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1859217924}[命令后，再]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[与]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[映射关系]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，此时，该命令的功能依然生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1859545604}[命令时，若其中]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[中部分]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不满足要求（比如指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不存在，指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[ VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[外的其他特殊类型]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，本端口已经在指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下的其他]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式等），则本命令只在其他满足要求的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中生效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1859152389}[命令与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**[及]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[port private-vlan trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令互斥。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](VLAN命令.files/image003.png){#图片 29 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1859283461}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[**[port private-vlan trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_38635873}[命令适用于需要多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（分别对应不同的]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[）]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[通过下行端口的情况，配置该命令后多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[携带]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[通过下行端口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}**[port private-vlan host]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[适用于只有一个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[通过下行端口的情况，配置该命令后]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不带]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}[Tag]{lang="EN-US"}[通过下行端口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_747226874}

[[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x911049541}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[的类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[关联，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 30]{lang="EN-US"}[关联，配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[在链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，然后取消该配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_640680434}[配置链路类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Access]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x839376339}[显示端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[当前配置，可以看出当该端口在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式时，该端口会加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 20]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[及关联的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[，同时修改链路类型。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- private-vlan （VLAN interface view）**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[private-vlan secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2082639994}[命令用来配置当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[间三层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710940339}[用来取消当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[下指定的或所有的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[间三层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1036817628}

[**[private-vlan secondary ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710874803}

[**[undo private-vlan ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **secondary** *vlan-id-list* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_990621302}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_711333555}

[[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x377730796}[间三层不互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_711268019}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1160254864}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710809264}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1686232782}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710743728}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_45508425}

[*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710678192}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[列表，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的范围。表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x729121224}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[该命令只能在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710612656}[对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口（]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN interface]{lang="EN-US"}[）视图下执行，且]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[必须]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[已经]{style="font-family:宋体"}[与该对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[建立]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[映射关系，同时要求]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不能建立对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[要使功能生效，还需要在该三层虚接口上配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址和本地代理]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ARP/ND]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[没有配置三层互通的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_20444025}[可以创建对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口，配置了三层互通的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[不能创建对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在同一]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN interface]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_711071408}[下多次执行本命令时，最终生效结果是多次配置的集合。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1425949378}[命令时，如果不带参数]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list*]{lang="EN-US"}[，就解除所有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[的三层互通；如果带有该参数，就解除指定的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[的三层互通。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_711005872}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x911049540}[下的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[三层互通（]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[为上行端口，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/4]{lang="EN-US"}[为下行端口）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_640614898}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1253798320}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1281177206}

[**[secondary]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vlan-id-list*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1939795993}[：表示方式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[ = { *vlan-id1* \[ **to** *vlan-id2* \] }&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan-id]{lang="EN-US"}*[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id2*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值要大于或等于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vlan]{lang="EN-US"}*[-*id1*]{lang="EN-US"}[的值，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[&\<1-10\>]{lang="EN-US"}[表示前面的参数最多可以重复输入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[次。其中，系统缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 1]{lang="EN-US"}[）虽然在取值范围中，但不能配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x843305908}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1267905308}[可以与多个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[建立映射关系。在同一]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[视图下多次执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令时，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[是多次配置的集合。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_801824945}[命令时，若已经]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[，且设备上有端口工作在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式或]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，则依据接口配置会触发配置同步]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo private-vlan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_710678193}[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[如果不带参数]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[secondary ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vlan-id-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[，就解除所有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系；如果带有该参数，就解除参数指定的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和对应]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345667754}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2089616248}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 3]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[private-vlan community]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345864362}[命令用来配置同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内各端口二层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x125800344}

[[同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x133946483}[内的端口二层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[private-vlan community]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1338120113}[命令实现的功能与]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令相同。当用户通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[save]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令保存配置时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[private-vlan community]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的配置不会被存入配置文件。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_599871938}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x911049543}[内各端口二层互通（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）。完成下述配置后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}[内端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[二层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_640549362}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**Private VLAN \-- Private VLAN配置命令 \-- private-vlan isolated**

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](VLAN命令.files/image001.png){#图片 1 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_553551800}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1651419475}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x241971228}[命令用来配置同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内各端口二层隔离，即配置该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[为隔离]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1659468212}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x203865985}

[**[private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1545418997}

[**[undo private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_490858546}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1156929047}

[[同一]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345274535}[内的端口能够二层互通。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_250507689}

[[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x80019291}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x380893270}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1476538160}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x653045856}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1462392166}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[添加]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_38701413}[到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[该]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[隔离]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary ]{lang="EN-US"}[VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的各下行端口二层隔离]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1612647328}[与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[建立映射关系，并且下行端口配置为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[trunk secondary]{lang="EN-US"}[模式]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[后，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[private-vlan isolated]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令才生效]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Secondary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1312638244}[隔离与]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Super VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Sub VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[配置互斥。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1345471143}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x911049544}[内各端口二层隔离（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[及]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[host]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）。完成下述配置后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}[内端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[二层隔离。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_640352754}[建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Primary VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Secondary VLAN 4]{lang="EN-US"}[的映射关系。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_640549367}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN 2]{lang="EN-US"}[中工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[promiscuous]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1111518899}

[*[minutes]{lang="PT-BR"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2018226685}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[的老化时间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="PT-BR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[43200]{lang="PT-BR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[单位为分钟。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x60887824}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在自动模式下，系统会根据收到的语音报文的源]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_413636281}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，来决定是否把该报文的入端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[。在将入端口加入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[后，系统会同时启动老化定时器。经过老化时间后，如果设备没有从该端口收到任何语音报文，老化时间到达后系统会自动把该端口从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中删除。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[老化时间只对]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_695724334}[工作在自动模式的端口有效，对]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在手工模式的端口无效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x256333443}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2024903771}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的老化时间配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[分钟。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2025423840}

[\[Sysname\] voice-vlan aging 100]{lang="EN-US"}

[]{#_Toc300652823}[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1445165648}

[*[mac-address]{lang="PT-BR"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1113407205}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[语音报文的源]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[格式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="PT-BR"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[如]{style="font-family:宋体"}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1234-1234-1234]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mask ]{lang="FR"}***[oui-mask]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x347571908}[：]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的有效长度，用掩码表示，格式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="EN-US"}[。由连续的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[和连续的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[组成，表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的匹配长度。如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FFFF-0000-0000]{lang="EN-US"}[，如果要匹配某个设备厂商的语音设备，可以设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FFFF-FF00-0000]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[description]{lang="EN-US"}***[ text]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2041742794}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的描述字符串，长度为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[oui]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_76172791}[：指定要删除的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，格式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="EN-US"}[，如]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1234-1200-0000]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址不能是广播地址或者组播地址，也不能是全]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[的地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址是]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[mac-address]{lang="FR"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[oui-mask]{lang="EN-US"}*[参数相与的结果。可以通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[display voice-vlan mac-address]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令查看当前系统支持的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="EN-US"}[地址的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x2136138852}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x998650607}[命令用来配置端口将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内语音报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为指定值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x446880153}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1722618317}

[**[voice-vlan qos ]{lang="EN-US"}***[cos-value dscp-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1872943222}

[**[undo voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x515659655}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566433536}

[[端口将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1434094059}[内语音报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[6]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[46]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1618066766}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_430207194}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1387953282}

[[network-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_479185836}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1132240231}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_907941210}

[*[cos-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_920140641}[：语音报文]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="PT-BR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[7]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[dscp-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_490809923}[：语音报文]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="PT-BR"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[63]{lang="PT-BR"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x432265127}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566368000}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[使能的情况下，不允许配置本命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[必须]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[后，才能配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[本命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果对于同一端口多次执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x789298291}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[voice-vlan qos trust]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[以]{style="font-family:宋体"}[最后配置的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[命令为准]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x701784216}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1969306641}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内的语音报文时，将报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[45]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1994840523}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] voice-vlan qos 5 45]{lang="EN-US"}

[]{#_Toc300652827}[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1028603338}

**Voice VLAN \-- Voice VLAN配置命令 \-- voice-vlan qos trust**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
