[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1606620078}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[voice-vlan qos trust]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566564608}[命令用来配置端口信任]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内语音报文的优先级，即端口不会修改]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内语音报文自带的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1302135001}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x651595934}

[**[voice-vlan qos trust]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_199600995}

[**[undo voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_2129084221}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x812624651}

[[端口将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x834362034}[内语音报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[6]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值修改为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[46]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1636119701}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_129134158}[通道接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/S]{lang="EN-US"}[通道聚合接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[二层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[逻辑接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1889999827}

[[network-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1731229700}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566499072}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1417626050}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1076105708}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[使能的情况下，不允许配置本命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[必须]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[功能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[后，才能配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[本命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果对于同一端口多次执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[voice-vlan qos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x84887479}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[voice-vlan qos trust]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[以最后]{style="font-family:宋体"}[配置的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[命令为准]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x354861203}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1564900978}[配置端口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[信任语音报文的优先级。端口收到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[内语音报文后，报文自带的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DSCP]{lang="EN-US"}[值保持不变。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x542289239}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] voice-vlan qos trust]{lang="EN-US"}

**Voice VLAN \-- Voice VLAN配置命令 \-- voice-vlan security enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[voice-vlan security enable]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1736099329}[命令用来使能]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[的安全模式。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[undo voice-vlan security enable]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566695680}[命令用来关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[的安全模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_949419362}

[**[voice-vlan security enable]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_35199171}

[**[undo voice-vlan security enable]{lang="PT-BR"}**]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1378326086}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x68672465}

[[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1562711931}[工作在安全模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1911867394}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1698268993}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1632774621}

[[network-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1913799243}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="PT-BR"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_566630144}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1821853823}

[[在安全模式下]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1303046692}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[中只能有语音流量。用户使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[的安全模式以后]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VLAN]{lang="PT-BR"}[的报文都经过过滤]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[设备会根据报文的源]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址是否匹配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OUI]{lang="PT-BR"}[地址来过滤所有非语音流量]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[增加安全性保证语音流的优先级]{style="font-family:宋体"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[保证通话质量。在普通模式下，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[中允许有业务流量和语音流量并存。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1503624915}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1526663660}[关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Voice VLAN]{lang="EN-US"}[的安全模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_x1065315076}

[\[Sysname\] undo voice-vlan security enable]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x1851_x4487_1637189160}

:::
